                    raise WorkflowAssetError(
                        "workflow_asset_version_unknown",
                        "规则版本不存在",
                        status_code=404,
                    )
                binding = bind_rule_revision(
                    db,
                    asset=row,
                    rule_revision=rule,
                    created_by=user.username,
                )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(binding)
            return _binding_payload(db, binding)
        except WorkflowAssetError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{asset_key}/prompt-versions", status_code=201)
    def create_prompt_draft(
        asset_key: str,
        payload: WorkflowAssetPromptDraftCreate,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_write(user)
        _require_write(user, "prompts:write")
        try:
            asset = _asset(db, payload.category_key, asset_key)
            existing = db.scalar(
                select(PromptVersion.id).where(
                    PromptVersion.category_key == payload.category_key,
                    PromptVersion.stage == payload.stage,
                    PromptVersion.version == payload.version,
                )
            )
            if existing is not None:
                raise WorkflowAssetError(
                    "workflow_prompt_version_conflict",
                    "当前类目的该提示词版本已经存在",
                    status_code=409,
                )
            prompt = PromptVersion(
                category_key=payload.category_key,
                pipeline_scope=payload.pipeline_scope,
                stage=payload.stage,
                name=payload.name,
                version=payload.version,
                system_prompt=payload.system_prompt,
                user_prompt=payload.user_prompt,
                rubric_version=payload.rubric_version,
                status="draft",
                source="manual",
                change_note=payload.change_note,
                created_by=user.username,
            )
            db.add(prompt)
            try:
                db.flush()
            except IntegrityError as exc:
                # 并发下两次先查后插都通过时，由 (category_key, stage, version) 唯一约束兜底。
                if not is_prompt_version_conflict(exc):
                    raise
                raise WorkflowAssetError(
                    "workflow_prompt_version_conflict",
                    "当前类目的该提示词版本已经存在",
                    status_code=409,
                ) from None
            binding = bind_prompt_version(
                db,
                asset=asset,
                prompt_version=prompt,
                created_by=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(prompt)
            db.refresh(binding)
            return {
                "prompt_version": {
                    "id": prompt.id,
                    "category_key": prompt.category_key,
                    "stage": prompt.stage,
                    "name": prompt.name,
                    "version": prompt.version,
                    "rubric_version": prompt.rubric_version,
                    "status": prompt.status,
                },
                "binding": _binding_payload(db, binding),
            }
        except WorkflowAssetError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_case_aggregation.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
from copy import deepcopy
import math
import re
from typing import Any

from .audit import canonical_json


_SAFE_DOT_PATH = re.compile(
    r"^\$\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*(?:\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*)*$"
)
_TERMINAL_STATUSES = frozenset({"succeeded", "failed", "skipped"})
_MISSING = object()


class WorkflowCaseAggregationError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _fail(code: str, message: str) -> None:
    raise WorkflowCaseAggregationError(code, message)


def _mapping(value: Any, *, code: str, message: str) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        _fail(code, message)
    return value


def _safe_path(value: Any, *, code: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or _SAFE_DOT_PATH.fullmatch(value) is None:
        _fail(code, "案例聚合只允许 $.字段.子字段 形式的安全对象路径")
    return value


def _resolve_path(value: Any, path: str) -> Any:
    current = value
    for key in path[2:].split("."):
        if not isinstance(current, Mapping) or key not in current:
            return _MISSING
        current = current[key]
    return current


def _finite_number(value: Any, *, code: str, message: str) -> float:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        _fail(code, message)
    normalized = float(value)
    if not math.isfinite(normalized):
        _fail(code, message)
    return normalized


CASE_SUMMARY_SCHEMA_VERSION = "workflow-case-aggregate-summary-v1"


def _number_label(value: float) -> str:
    return str(int(value)) if value.is_integer() else format(value, ".15g")


def _feature_summary_text(feature: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    """把一个特征的取值计数写成「实景照片有15张,技术图有1张」。"""

    parts = []
    for entry in feature.get("values") or []:
        value = entry.get("value")
        label = value if isinstance(value, str) else canonical_json(value)
        parts.append(f"{label}有{entry.get('count')}张")
    return ",".join(parts)


def _score_summary_text(distribution: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    """把分数分布写成「82分有3张,84分有3张」，按分数升序而不是字典序。"""

    def sort_key(label: str) -> tuple[int, float | str]:
        try:
            return (0, float(label))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return (1, label)

    return ",".join(
        f"{label}分有{distribution[label]}张"
        for label in sorted(distribution, key=sort_key)
        if distribution[label]
    )


def build_case_aggregate_summary(aggregate: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """产出运营可读的素材维度聚合摘要。

    运营的素材维度样本就是这三行（2026-09-04）：
        图片总张图：16张
        所有表现图的分数：82分有3张,84分有3张,85分有3张,86分有1张,87分有1张,88分有4张
        所有图片的类型：实景照片有15张,技术图有1张
    结构化数据一直都在聚合结果里，这里只是按同一口径落成可读文本，
    让运营在结果页/纠偏页直接看懂，也让导入的历史样本能和平台产出对齐。
    """

    counts = aggregate.get("counts") or {}
    score = aggregate.get("score") or {}
    features = aggregate.get("features") or {}
    total = counts.get("included")
    if not isinstance(total, int):
        total = counts.get("source_total") if isinstance(counts.get("source_total"), int) else 0
    lines = {
        "image_total_text": f"{total}张",
        "score_distribution_text": _score_summary_text(score.get("distribution") or {}),
    }
    feature_texts = {}
    feature_counts: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    for name in sorted(features):
        entry = features[name]
        if not isinstance(entry, Mapping):
            continue
        feature_texts[name] = _feature_summary_text(entry)
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        for value_entry in entry.get("values") or []:
            value = value_entry.get("value")
            label = value if isinstance(value, str) else canonical_json(value)
            counts[label] = counts.get(label, 0) + int(value_entry.get("count") or 0)
        feature_counts[name] = counts
    score_distribution = {
        str(label): int(count)
        for label, count in (score.get("distribution") or {}).items()
        if count
    }
    # 与导入的历史样本共用一套键名，存量回归才能逐项对齐。
    return {
        "schema_version": CASE_SUMMARY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "image_total": total,
        **lines,
        "score_distribution": score_distribution,
        "scored_images": sum(score_distribution.values()),
        "feature_texts": feature_texts,
        "feature_counts": feature_counts,
    }


def compare_case_summary(
    expected: Mapping[str, Any],
    actual: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    features: Iterable[str] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把历史样本的期望摘要与平台产出的聚合摘要逐项对齐。

    只比结构化计数，不比文本：文本是同一份数据的展示形式，分隔符或顺序
    差异不该判成不一致。
    """

    diffs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def _compare(field: str, left: Any, right: Any) -> None:
        if left != right:
            diffs.append({"field": field, "expected": left, "actual": right})

    _compare("image_total", int(expected.get("image_total") or 0), int(actual.get("image_total") or 0))
    _compare(
        "score_distribution",
        {str(k): int(v) for k, v in (expected.get("score_distribution") or {}).items() if v},
        {str(k): int(v) for k, v in (actual.get("score_distribution") or {}).items() if v},
    )
    expected_features = expected.get("feature_counts") or {}
    actual_features = actual.get("feature_counts") or {}
    names = (
        list(features)
        if features is not None
        # 只比双方都给了的特征：样本表只统计了类型，不该因为平台多算了风格就判不一致。
        else sorted(set(expected_features) & set(actual_features))
    )
    for name in names:
        _compare(
            f"feature_counts.{name}",
            {str(k): int(v) for k, v in (expected_features.get(name) or {}).items() if v},
            {str(k): int(v) for k, v in (actual_features.get(name) or {}).items() if v},
        )
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-case-summary-comparison-v1",
        "matched": not diffs,
        "compared_features": names,
        "diffs": diffs,
    }


def _validate_contract(contract: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    foreach_node_id = contract.get("foreach_node_id")
    if not isinstance(foreach_node_id, str) or not foreach_node_id.strip():
        _fail("aggregate_foreach_node_invalid", "案例聚合缺少逐图节点身份")

    included = contract.get("included_statuses")
    if (
        not isinstance(included, list)
        or not included
        or any(status not in _TERMINAL_STATUSES for status in included)
        or len(included) != len(set(included))
    ):
        _fail("aggregate_statuses_invalid", "案例聚合状态集合不合法")

    score_path = _safe_path(
        contract.get("score_path"),
        code="aggregate_score_path_invalid",
    )
    feature_paths = contract.get("feature_paths")
    if not isinstance(feature_paths, Mapping) or len(feature_paths) > 64:
        _fail(
            "aggregate_feature_paths_invalid",
            "案例聚合特征路径必须是最多 64 项的命名对象",
        )
    normalized_features: dict[str, str] = {}
    for raw_name, raw_path in feature_paths.items():
        if (
            not isinstance(raw_name, str)
            or not raw_name.strip()
            or raw_name != raw_name.strip()
        ):
            _fail(
                "aggregate_feature_paths_invalid",
                "案例聚合特征名称必须是非空稳定文本",
            )
        normalized_features[raw_name] = _safe_path(
            raw_path,
            code="aggregate_feature_path_invalid",
        )

    empty_policy = contract.get("empty_policy")
    if empty_policy not in {"fail", "emit_empty"}:
        _fail("aggregate_empty_policy_invalid", "案例聚合空集合策略不合法")
    failure_projection = contract.get("failure_projection")
    if failure_projection not in {"include", "exclude"}:
        _fail(
            "aggregate_failure_projection_invalid",
            "案例聚合失败项投影策略不合法",
        )

    return {
        "foreach_node_id": foreach_node_id,
        "included_statuses": frozenset(included),
        "score_path": score_path,
        "feature_paths": dict(sorted(normalized_features.items())),
        "score_buckets": deepcopy(contract.get("score_buckets")),
        "empty_policy": empty_policy,
        "failure_projection": failure_projection,
    }


def _source(
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    foreach_node_id: str,
) -> tuple[Mapping[str, Any], list[Mapping[str, Any]]]:
    upstream = _mapping(
        input_manifest.get("upstream"),
        code="aggregate_upstream_invalid",
        message="案例聚合输入缺少冻结上游对象",
    )
    source = _mapping(
        upstream.get(foreach_node_id),
        code="aggregate_foreach_output_missing",
        message=f"案例聚合输入缺少逐图节点：{foreach_node_id}",
    )
    if source.get("schema_version") == "workflow-node-result-v1":
        if (
            source.get("status") != "succeeded"
            or source.get("node_instance_id") != foreach_node_id
        ):
            _fail("aggregate_source_envelope_invalid", "逐图冻结输出信封不合法")
        source = _mapping(
            source.get("output"),
            code="aggregate_foreach_output_missing",
            message="逐图冻结输出信封缺少业务结果",
        )
    if source.get("schema_version") != "workflow-foreach-output-v1":
        _fail("aggregate_source_schema_invalid", "逐图冻结输出版本不受支持")
    if source.get("node_instance_id") != foreach_node_id:
        _fail("aggregate_source_node_mismatch", "逐图冻结输出节点身份不匹配")
    items = source.get("items")
    if not isinstance(items, list):
        _fail("aggregate_source_items_invalid", "逐图冻结输出缺少子项数组")
    counts = _mapping(
        source.get("counts"),
        code="aggregate_source_counts_invalid",
        message="逐图冻结输出缺少状态计数",
    )

    normalized: list[Mapping[str, Any]] = []
    seen_keys: set[str] = set()
    seen_ordinals: set[int] = set()
    actual = Counter()
    for raw_item in items:
        item = _mapping(
            raw_item,
            code="aggregate_source_item_invalid",
            message="逐图冻结输出子项必须是对象",
        )
        item_key = item.get("item_key")
        ordinal = item.get("ordinal")
        status = item.get("status")
        if not isinstance(item_key, str) or not item_key:
            _fail("aggregate_source_item_key_invalid", "逐图子项身份不合法")
        if (
            isinstance(ordinal, bool)
            or not isinstance(ordinal, int)
            or ordinal < 0
        ):
            _fail("aggregate_source_ordinal_invalid", "逐图子项顺序不合法")
        if status not in _TERMINAL_STATUSES:
            _fail("aggregate_source_status_invalid", "逐图子项不是终态")
        if item_key in seen_keys or ordinal in seen_ordinals:
            _fail("aggregate_source_identity_duplicate", "逐图子项身份或顺序重复")
        seen_keys.add(item_key)
        seen_ordinals.add(ordinal)
        actual[status] += 1
        if status == "succeeded" and not isinstance(item.get("output"), Mapping):
            _fail("aggregate_source_output_invalid", "成功逐图子项缺少结构化输出")
        if status != "succeeded" and not isinstance(item.get("error"), Mapping):
            _fail("aggregate_source_error_invalid", "失败或跳过子项缺少错误事实")
        normalized.append(item)

    expected_counts = {
        "total": len(normalized),
        "succeeded": actual["succeeded"],
        "failed": actual["failed"],
        "skipped": actual["skipped"],
    }
    if any(counts.get(key) != value for key, value in expected_counts.items()):
        _fail("aggregate_source_counts_mismatch", "逐图冻结状态计数与子项不一致")
    return source, sorted(
        normalized,
        key=lambda item: (int(item["ordinal"]), str(item["item_key"])),
    )


def _feature_values(value: Any) -> list[Any]:
    values: Sequence[Any]
    if isinstance(value, list):
        values = value
    else:
        values = [value]
    normalized: list[Any] = []
    for item in values:
        if isinstance(item, (Mapping, list)):
            _fail(
                "aggregate_feature_value_invalid",
                "案例聚合特征只允许标量或标量数组",
            )
        if isinstance(item, float) and not math.isfinite(item):
            _fail(
                "aggregate_feature_value_invalid",
                "案例聚合特征数值必须有限",
            )
        normalized.append(deepcopy(item))
    return normalized


def normalize_score_buckets(
    definition: Any,
) -> list[tuple[str, float, float]] | None:
    if definition is None:
        return None
    if not isinstance(definition, list) or not definition:
        _fail(
            "aggregate_score_buckets_invalid",
            "分数区间必须是非空区间数组",
        )
    buckets: list[tuple[str, float, float]] = []
    labels: set[str] = set()
    for raw in definition:
        bucket = _mapping(
            raw,
            code="aggregate_score_buckets_invalid",
            message="每个分数区间必须是对象",
        )
        if set(bucket) != {"label", "min_inclusive", "max_exclusive"}:
            _fail(
                "aggregate_score_buckets_invalid",
                "分数区间只允许 label、min_inclusive、max_exclusive",
            )
        label = bucket.get("label")
        if not isinstance(label, str) or not label.strip() or label in labels:
            _fail("aggregate_score_buckets_invalid", "分数区间名称不合法或重复")
        minimum = _finite_number(
            bucket.get("min_inclusive"),
            code="aggregate_score_buckets_invalid",
            message="分数区间下界必须是有限数值",
        )
        maximum = _finite_number(
            bucket.get("max_exclusive"),
            code="aggregate_score_buckets_invalid",
            message="分数区间上界必须是有限数值",
        )
        if minimum >= maximum:
            _fail("aggregate_score_buckets_invalid", "分数区间下界必须小于上界")
        labels.add(label)
        buckets.append((label, minimum, maximum))
    ordered = sorted(buckets, key=lambda value: (value[1], value[2], value[0]))
    if any(left[2] > right[1] for left, right in zip(ordered, ordered[1:])):
        _fail("aggregate_score_buckets_invalid", "分数区间不得重叠")
    return ordered


def _distribution(scores: list[float], definition: Any) -> dict[str, int]:
    ordered = normalize_score_buckets(definition)
    if ordered is None:
        counts = Counter(_number_label(score) for score in scores)
        return dict(sorted(counts.items()))
    counts = {label: 0 for label, _minimum, _maximum in ordered}
    for score in scores:
        matched = [
            label
            for label, minimum, maximum in ordered
            if minimum <= score < maximum
        ]
        if len(matched) != 1:
            _fail(
                "aggregate_score_bucket_unmatched",
                f"分数 {_number_label(score)} 未被唯一分数区间覆盖",
            )
        counts[matched[0]] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))


def _base_projection(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "item_key": item["item_key"],
        "ordinal": item["ordinal"],
        "status": item["status"],
        "attempt_count": item.get("attempt_count", 0),
        "input_hash": item.get("input_hash"),
    }


def aggregate_case(
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    contract: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Deterministically aggregate one frozen ``foreach_image`` output."""

    if not isinstance(input_manifest, Mapping) or not isinstance(contract, Mapping):
        _fail("aggregate_input_invalid", "案例聚合输入和合同必须是对象")
    config = _validate_contract(contract)
    source, items = _source(
        input_manifest,
        foreach_node_id=config["foreach_node_id"],
    )
    included = [
        item for item in items if item["status"] in config["included_statuses"]
    ]
    if not included and config["empty_policy"] == "fail":
        _fail("aggregate_case_empty", "案例聚合没有符合冻结状态合同的子项")

    scores: list[float] = []
    feature_counts: dict[str, Counter[str]] = {
        name: Counter() for name in config["feature_paths"]
    }
    feature_originals: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {
        name: {} for name in config["feature_paths"]
    }
    feature_observed = Counter()
    feature_missing = Counter()
    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    for item in included:
        projected = _base_projection(item)
        if item["status"] != "succeeded":
            projected["error"] = deepcopy(dict(item["error"]))
            results.append(projected)
            continue
        output = item["output"]
        score_value = _resolve_path(output, config["score_path"])
        score = _finite_number(
            score_value,
            code="aggregate_score_invalid",
            message=(
                f"逐图子项 {item['item_key']} 的聚合分数缺失、非数值或不是有限数"
            ),
        )
        scores.append(score)
        projected_features: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for name, path in config["feature_paths"].items():
            raw_value = _resolve_path(output, path)
            if raw_value is _MISSING:
                feature_missing[name] += 1
                projected_features[name] = None
                continue
            feature_observed[name] += 1
            projected_features[name] = deepcopy(raw_value)
            for feature_value in _feature_values(raw_value):
                key = canonical_json(feature_value)
                feature_counts[name][key] += 1
                feature_originals[name][key] = feature_value
        projected.update(
            {
                "output_hash": item.get("output_hash"),
                "score": score,
                "features": projected_features,
                "output": deepcopy(dict(output)),
            }
        )
        results.append(projected)

    failures = []
    if config["failure_projection"] == "include":
        for item in items:
            if item["status"] == "succeeded":
                continue
            failure = _base_projection(item)
            failure["error"] = deepcopy(dict(item["error"]))
            failures.append(failure)

    features = {}
    for name in config["feature_paths"]:
        features[name] = {
            "observed_items": feature_observed[name],
            "missing_items": feature_missing[name],
            "values": [
                {
                    "value": deepcopy(feature_originals[name][key]),
                    "count": feature_counts[name][key],
                }
                for key in sorted(feature_counts[name])
            ],
        }

    source_counts = source["counts"]
    result: dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-case-aggregate-v1",
        "foreach_node_id": config["foreach_node_id"],
        "source_hash": source.get("source_hash"),
        "item_contract_hash": source.get("item_contract_hash"),
        "counts": {
            "source_total": source_counts["total"],
            "included": len(included),
            "succeeded": source_counts["succeeded"],
            "failed": source_counts["failed"],
            "skipped": source_counts["skipped"],
        },
        "score": {
            "count": len(scores),
            "mean": math.fsum(scores) / len(scores) if scores else None,
            "minimum": min(scores) if scores else None,
            "maximum": max(scores) if scores else None,
            "distribution": _distribution(scores, config["score_buckets"]),
        },
        "features": features,
        "results": results,
        "failures": failures,
    }
    return result

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_composer_runtime.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import ScriptDefinition, ScriptVersion


COMPOSER_FIXTURE_VERSION = "1"
COMPOSER_RUNTIME_STEP_TYPES = {
    "upstream_connector": "connector",
    "transform": "transform",
    "prompt": "model_call",
    "foreach_image": "model_call",
    "rule_module": "rule_eval",
    "rule_contract": "rule_eval",
    "level_matcher": "rule_eval",
    "human_task": "human_task",
    "downstream_projection": "projection",
    "image_first_seen_lookup": "connector",
    "branch": "transform",
    "join": "reconcile",
    "reconcile": "reconcile",
    "aggregate_case": "reconcile",
}


def composer_fixture_reference(kind: str) -> str:
    return f"fixture.composer.{kind}@{COMPOSER_FIXTURE_VERSION}"


def composer_runtime_step_type(kind: str) -> str:
    return COMPOSER_RUNTIME_STEP_TYPES[kind]


def ensure_composer_fixture_version(db: Session, kind: str) -> ScriptVersion:
    script_key = f"fixture.composer.{kind}"
    definition = db.scalar(
        select(ScriptDefinition).where(ScriptDefinition.script_key == script_key)
    )
    if definition is None:
        definition = ScriptDefinition(
            script_key=script_key,
            name=f"工作流编辑器 dry-run · {kind}",
            description="工作流编辑器节点的受控 deterministic noop fixture",
            owner="label-platform",
            allowed_categories_json="[]",
            step_types_json=canonical_json([composer_runtime_step_type(kind)]),
            status="active",
            created_by="workflow-composer",
        )
        db.add(definition)
        db.flush()
    version = db.scalar(
        select(ScriptVersion).where(
            ScriptVersion.script_definition_id == definition.id,
            ScriptVersion.version == COMPOSER_FIXTURE_VERSION,
        )
    )
    if version is None:
        artifact = hashlib.sha256(
            composer_fixture_reference(kind).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        version = ScriptVersion(
            script_definition_id=definition.id,
            version=COMPOSER_FIXTURE_VERSION,
            display_name=f"{kind} deterministic dry-run v1",
            executor_kind="deterministic_fixture",
            artifact_sha256=artifact,
            manifest_json=canonical_json(
                {"fixture": "noop", "composer_kind": kind}
            ),
            input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
            output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
            required_permissions_json="[]",
            idempotency_template="{run_key}:{step_key}:{input_hash}",
            timeout_seconds=60,
            max_attempts=2,
            retry_policy_json='{"kind":"fixed","delay_seconds":0}',
            concurrency_limit=1,
            estimated_cost_json='{"currency":"CNY","micros":0}',
            status="active",
            validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
            blocked_reason="",
            created_by="workflow-composer",
        )
        db.add(version)
        db.flush()
    return version


def composer_runtime_steps(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    schema_version = manifest.get("schema_version")
    return [
        {
            "key": (
                node["node_instance_id"]
                if schema_version == "workflow-v3"
                else node["key"]
            ),
            "type": composer_runtime_step_type(node["kind"]),
            "script_version": composer_fixture_reference(node["kind"]),
            "input_schema": node["input_schema"],
            "output_schema": node["output_schema"],
        }
        for node in manifest["nodes"]
    ]

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_conditions.py =====
"""Safe declarative conditions shared by workflow validation and execution."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Collection, Mapping, Sequence

from .audit import canonical_json


CONDITION_OPERATORS = frozenset(
    {"eq", "neq", "in", "not_in", "exists", "gte", "gt", "lte", "lt"}
)
_COMPOUND_OPERATORS = ("all", "any", "not")
_LEAF_FIELDS = frozenset({"op", "path", "value"})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ConditionError:
    path: str
    code: str
    message: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ConditionEvaluation:
    matched: bool
    referenced_facts: Mapping[str, Any]
    condition_hash: str


class WorkflowConditionError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, error: ConditionError):
        super().__init__(error.message)
        self.path = error.path
        self.code = error.code


def _append_error(
    errors: list[ConditionError], path: str, code: str, message: str
) -> None:
    errors.append(ConditionError(path=path, code=code, message=message))


def validate_condition(
    condition: Any,
    *,
    allowed_steps: Collection[str],
    allowed_roots: Collection[str] = ("steps",),
    path: str = "condition",
    _depth: int = 0,
) -> list[ConditionError]:
    """Validate a condition without evaluating code or reading external state."""

    errors: list[ConditionError] = []
    if _depth > 8:
        _append_error(
            errors,
            path,
            "condition_depth_exceeded",
            "条件嵌套深度不能超过 8",
        )
        return errors
    if not isinstance(condition, Mapping):
        _append_error(
            errors,
            path,
            "condition_object_required",
            "条件必须是对象",
        )
        return errors

    compound = [key for key in _COMPOUND_OPERATORS if key in condition]
    if compound:
        if len(compound) != 1 or len(condition) != 1:
            _append_error(
                errors,
                path,
                "condition_shape_invalid",
                "复合条件只能声明 all、any 或 not 之一",
            )
            return errors
        operator = compound[0]
        value = condition[operator]
        if operator == "not":
            children: Sequence[Any] = [value]
        elif isinstance(value, list) and value:
            children = value
        else:
            _append_error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.{operator}",
                "condition_children_required",
                f"{operator} 必须包含非空条件数组",
            )
            return errors
        for index, child in enumerate(children):
            errors.extend(
                validate_condition(
                    child,
                    allowed_steps=allowed_steps,
                    allowed_roots=allowed_roots,
                    path=f"{path}.{operator}[{index}]",
                    _depth=_depth + 1,
                )
            )
        return errors

    for field in sorted(set(condition) - _LEAF_FIELDS):
        _append_error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.{field}",
            "condition_field_unknown",
            "条件包含未批准字段",
        )

    operator = condition.get("op")
    if operator not in CONDITION_OPERATORS:
        _append_error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.op",
            "condition_operator_unsupported",
            "条件操作符不在允许列表",
        )

    reference = condition.get("path")
    if not isinstance(reference, str) or not reference.strip():
        _append_error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.path",
            "condition_path_invalid",
            "条件必须引用批准的冻结事实路径",
        )
        return errors
    parts = reference.split(".")
    allowed_root_set = set(allowed_roots)
    if parts[0] not in allowed_root_set:
        _append_error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.path",
            "condition_path_invalid",
            "条件路径不在批准的冻结事实范围内",
        )
        return errors
    if parts[0] == "steps" and (
        len(parts) < 4
        or parts[1] not in set(allowed_steps)
        or parts[2] != "output"
    ):
        _append_error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.path",
            "condition_path_unknown",
            "条件只能引用可达前序步骤的输出字段",
        )
    elif parts[0] != "steps" and len(parts) < 2:
        _append_error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.path",
            "condition_path_unknown",
            "条件路径必须包含具体冻结事实字段",
        )

    if operator != "exists" and "value" not in condition:
        _append_error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.value",
            "condition_value_required",
            "该条件操作符必须声明比较值",
        )
    if operator in {"in", "not_in"} and not isinstance(
        condition.get("value"), list
    ):
        _append_error(
            errors,
            f"{path}.value",
            "condition_value_invalid",
            "包含判断的比较值必须是数组",
        )
    if operator in {"gte", "gt", "lte", "lt"}:
        value = condition.get("value")
        if not isinstance(value, (int, float)) or isinstance(value, bool):
            _append_error(
                errors,
                f"{path}.value",
                "condition_value_invalid",
                "数值比较必须使用数字",
            )
    return errors


def _lookup_fact(facts: Mapping[str, Any], reference: str) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
    current: Any = facts
    for part in reference.split("."):
        if not isinstance(current, Mapping) or part not in current:
            return False, None
        current = current[part]
    return True, current


def _evaluate_leaf(
    condition: Mapping[str, Any],
    facts: Mapping[str, Any],
    referenced: dict[str, Any],
) -> bool:
    reference = str(condition["path"])
    found, actual = _lookup_fact(facts, reference)
    referenced[reference] = actual if found else None
    operator = condition["op"]
    if operator == "exists":
        return found
    if not found:
        return False
    expected = condition.get("value")
    if operator == "eq":
        return actual == expected
    if operator == "neq":
        return actual != expected
    if operator == "in":
        return actual in expected
    if operator == "not_in":
        return actual not in expected
    if (
        not isinstance(actual, (int, float))
        or isinstance(actual, bool)
        or not isinstance(expected, (int, float))
        or isinstance(expected, bool)
    ):
        return False
    if operator == "gte":
        return actual >= expected
    if operator == "gt":
        return actual > expected
    if operator == "lte":
        return actual <= expected
    return actual < expected


def _evaluate(
    condition: Mapping[str, Any],
    facts: Mapping[str, Any],
    referenced: dict[str, Any],
) -> bool:
    if "all" in condition:
        values = [_evaluate(child, facts, referenced) for child in condition["all"]]
        return all(values)
    if "any" in condition:
        values = [_evaluate(child, facts, referenced) for child in condition["any"]]
        return any(values)
    if "not" in condition:
        return not _evaluate(condition["not"], facts, referenced)
    return _evaluate_leaf(condition, facts, referenced)


def evaluate_condition(
    condition: Mapping[str, Any], facts: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> ConditionEvaluation:
    """Evaluate a validated condition against an already-frozen fact mapping."""

    steps = facts.get("steps")
    allowed_steps = set(steps) if isinstance(steps, Mapping) else set()
    errors = validate_condition(
        condition,
        allowed_steps=allowed_steps,
        allowed_roots={"steps", "input"},
    )
    if errors:
        raise WorkflowConditionError(errors[0])
    referenced: dict[str, Any] = {}
    matched = _evaluate(condition, facts, referenced)
    condition_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(condition).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return ConditionEvaluation(
        matched=matched,
        referenced_facts={key: referenced[key] for key in sorted(referenced)},
        condition_hash=condition_hash,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_connector_executor.py =====
"""Frozen connector contracts for native workflow execution.

External reads happen before a workflow run becomes runnable and external writes
happen after a downstream projection has committed to the transactional outbox.
The node executor therefore records the selected contract and I/O boundary; it
never opens a network connection itself.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document

from copy import deepcopy
import hashlib
import json
from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .incremental_input import (
    IncrementalInputError,
    validate_frozen_content_ingress_input,
)
from .models import (
    ContentIngressEvent,
    ContentRecord,
    ConnectorContractVersion,
    MaterialPackage,
    MaterialPackageItem,
    ProductionRun,
    UpstreamIntakeEvent,
    WorkflowVersion,
)


class WorkflowConnectorError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        status_code: int = 409,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


def _json_object(raw: str, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        value = json.loads(raw or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "connector_contract_snapshot_invalid",
            f"连接器合同的{label}损坏",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "connector_contract_snapshot_invalid",
            f"连接器合同的{label}不是对象",
        )
    return value


def connector_contract_snapshot(
    contract: ConnectorContractVersion,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the credential-free contract frozen into a production run."""

    return {
        "schema_version": "connector-contract-snapshot-v1",
        "connector_contract_id": contract.id,
        "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
        "contract_version": contract.version,
        "direction": contract.direction,
        "category_key": contract.category_key,
        "logical_locator": contract.logical_locator,
        "adapter_key": contract.adapter_key,
        "schema_fingerprint": contract.schema_fingerprint,
        "field_mapping": _json_object(
            contract.field_mapping_json,
            label="字段映射",
        ),
        "cursor_contract": _json_object(
            contract.cursor_contract_json,
            label="游标规则",
        ),
        "idempotency_contract": _json_object(
            contract.idempotency_contract_json,
            label="幂等规则",
        ),
        "batch_size": contract.batch_size,
        "timeout_seconds": contract.timeout_seconds,
        "contract_hash": contract.contract_hash,
    }


def _selected_contract(
    db: Session,
    *,
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    connector_key: str,
    direction: str,
    category_key: str,
    required: bool,
    upstream_event_contract_version_id: int | None,
    upstream_event_contract_key: str | None,
) -> ConnectorContractVersion | None:
    configured_id = config.get("connector_contract_version_id")
    existing_snapshot = config.get("connector_snapshot")
    if configured_id is None and isinstance(existing_snapshot, Mapping):
        configured_id = existing_snapshot.get("connector_contract_id")
    event_override = (
        direction == "upstream"
        and upstream_event_contract_version_id is not None
        and upstream_event_contract_key == connector_key
    )
    if event_override:
        configured_id = upstream_event_contract_version_id

    # Historical stock workflows only carried a logical placeholder. Registering
    # a deployment contract later must not change their replay snapshot.
    if configured_id is None and not required:
        return None

    contract: ConnectorContractVersion | None
    if configured_id is not None:
        if isinstance(configured_id, bool) or not isinstance(configured_id, int):
            raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                "connector_contract_version_invalid",
                "接口节点选择的合同版本无效",
            )
        contract = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, configured_id)
    else:
        contract = db.scalar(
            select(ConnectorContractVersion)
            .where(
                ConnectorContractVersion.contract_key == connector_key,
                ConnectorContractVersion.direction == direction,
                ConnectorContractVersion.category_key == category_key,
                ConnectorContractVersion.status == "active",
            )
            .order_by(ConnectorContractVersion.version.desc())
            .limit(1)
        )

    if contract is None:
        if required:
            raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                "connector_contract_version_missing",
                "增量工作流的接口节点尚未选择可用合同版本",
            )
        return None
    if (
        contract.contract_key != connector_key
        or contract.direction != direction
        or contract.category_key != category_key
    ):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "connector_contract_version_mismatch",
            "接口节点选择的合同版本与类目、方向或逻辑接口不一致",
        )
    if required and contract.status != "active" and not event_override:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "connector_contract_version_inactive",
            "增量工作流只能选择已启用的接口合同版本",
        )
    return contract


def freeze_workflow_connector_contracts(
    db: Session,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    runtime_context: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Resolve connector selections and freeze them without physical details."""

    frozen = deepcopy(dict(manifest))
    nodes = frozen.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return frozen
    category_key = frozen.get("category_key")
    run_mode = runtime_context.get("run_mode")
    required = run_mode == "incremental"
    event_contract_id = runtime_context.get(
        "upstream_intake_contract_version_id"
    )
    event_contract_key = runtime_context.get("upstream_intake_contract_key")
    if event_contract_id is not None and (
        isinstance(event_contract_id, bool)
        or not isinstance(event_contract_id, int)
    ):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_contract_invalid",
            "上游接入事实的合同版本无效",
        )
    if not isinstance(category_key, str) or not category_key:
        if required:
            raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                "connector_category_missing",
                "增量工作流缺少业务类目标识",
            )
        return frozen

    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            continue
        kind = node.get("kind")
        if kind not in {"upstream_connector", "downstream_projection"}:
            continue
        direction = "upstream" if kind == "upstream_connector" else "downstream"
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, dict):
            if required:
                raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                    "connector_node_config_invalid",
                    "增量工作流的接口节点配置损坏",
                )
            continue
        connector_key = config.get("connector_key")
        if (
            not isinstance(connector_key, str)
            or not connector_key.strip()
            or ".unconfigured." in connector_key
        ):
            if required:
                raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                    "connector_contract_version_missing",
                    "增量工作流的接口节点尚未配置逻辑合同",
                )
            continue
        contract = _selected_contract(
            db,
            config=config,
            connector_key=connector_key,
            direction=direction,
            category_key=category_key,
            required=required,
            upstream_event_contract_version_id=(
                event_contract_id if isinstance(event_contract_id, int) else None
            ),
            upstream_event_contract_key=(
                event_contract_key if isinstance(event_contract_key, str) else None
            ),
        )
        if contract is None:
            config.pop("connector_snapshot", None)
            continue
        config["connector_contract_version_id"] = contract.id
        config["connector_snapshot"] = connector_contract_snapshot(contract)
    return frozen


def connector_node_evidence(
    *,
    run_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Describe the I/O boundary without changing a node's business payload."""

    runtime_context = run_snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    runtime_context = (
        runtime_context if isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping) else {}
    )
    run_mode = runtime_context.get("run_mode")
    kind = node.get("kind")
    config = node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    snapshot = config.get("connector_snapshot")
    snapshot = snapshot if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) else {}
    if run_mode == "stock_regression":
        io_mode = (
            "imported_sample"
            if kind == "upstream_connector"
            else "evidence_only"
        )
    elif run_mode == "incremental":
        io_mode = (
            "continuation_event"
            if kind == "upstream_connector"
            and config.get("input_mode", "initial_intake")
            == "continuation_event"
            else "intake_event"
            if kind == "upstream_connector"
            else "transactional_outbox"
        )
    else:
        io_mode = (
            "workflow_input"
            if kind == "upstream_connector"
            else "projection_only"
        )

    payload_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(dict(payload)).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    if (
        run_mode == "incremental"
        and kind == "upstream_connector"
        and config.get("input_mode", "initial_intake") != "continuation_event"
    ):
        expected_contract_id = runtime_context.get(
            "upstream_intake_contract_version_id"
        )
        expected_payload_hash = runtime_context.get("upstream_intake_payload_hash")
        if (
            not isinstance(snapshot.get("connector_contract_id"), int)
            or snapshot.get("connector_contract_id") != expected_contract_id
            or not isinstance(expected_payload_hash, str)
            or expected_payload_hash != payload_hash
        ):
            raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                "upstream_intake_snapshot_mismatch",
                "工作流输入与冻结的上游接入事实不一致",
            )
    return {
        "connector_contract_id": snapshot.get("connector_contract_id"),
        "contract_version": snapshot.get("contract_version"),
        "schema_fingerprint": snapshot.get("schema_fingerprint"),
        "io_mode": io_mode,
        "payload_hash": payload_hash,
        # Reads were completed by intake; writes are deferred to the outbox.
        "external_io_performed": False,
    }


def validate_incremental_intake_binding(
    db: Session,
    *,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    runtime_context: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    source_type: str | None,
    source_id: int | None,
    category_key: str | None,
    idempotency_key: str,
) -> None:
    """Fail closed when an incremental read node is not backed by intake."""

    if runtime_context.get("run_mode") != "incremental":
        return
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    upstream_nodes = [
        node
        for node in nodes or []
        if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "upstream_connector"
    ]
    if not upstream_nodes:
        return
    if source_type == "content_ingress_event" and isinstance(source_id, int):
        event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, source_id)
        if event is None or event.status != "applied":
            raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                "content_ingress_binding_required",
                "增量工作流绑定的内容接入事实不存在或尚不可运行",
            )
        record = db.get(ContentRecord, event.content_record_id)
        package_id = runtime_context.get("material_package_id")
        package = (
            db.get(MaterialPackage, package_id)
            if isinstance(package_id, int) and not isinstance(package_id, bool)
            else None
        )
        try:
            mechanism = json.loads(
                package.mechanism_snapshot_json if package is not None else "{}"
            )
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                "content_ingress_binding_mismatch",
                "增量素材包冻结输入损坏",
            ) from exc
        frozen_input = (
            mechanism.get("input_manifest")
            if isinstance(mechanism, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        frozen_input_hash = (
            mechanism.get("input_manifest_hash")
            if isinstance(mechanism, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        frozen_asset_id = (
            frozen_input.get("asset_id")
            if isinstance(frozen_input, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        item = (
            db.scalar(
                select(MaterialPackageItem).where(
                    MaterialPackageItem.package_id == package.id,
                    MaterialPackageItem.asset_id == frozen_asset_id,
                )
            )
            if package is not None
            and isinstance(frozen_asset_id, int)
            and not isinstance(frozen_asset_id, bool)
            else None
        )
        contract_id = runtime_context.get("upstream_intake_contract_version_id")
        contract_key = runtime_context.get("upstream_intake_contract_key")
        contract = (
            db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, contract_id)
            if isinstance(contract_id, int)
            else None
        )
        connector_keys = {
            config.get("connector_key")
            for node in upstream_nodes
            if isinstance((config := node.get("config")), Mapping)
        }
        input_hash = hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(dict(input_manifest)).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        if (
            package is None
            or package.status != "incremental_pending"
            or item is None
            or not isinstance(frozen_input, Mapping)
            or not isinstance(frozen_input_hash, str)
            or canonical_json(dict(input_manifest))
            != canonical_json(dict(frozen_input))
        ):
            raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                "content_ingress_binding_mismatch",
                "增量工作流输入与素材包冻结输入不一致",
            )
        try:
            validate_frozen_content_ingress_input(
                db,
                event=event,
                input_manifest=frozen_input,
                input_manifest_hash=frozen_input_hash,
                package_category_key=package.category_key,
                package_item_asset_id=item.asset_id,
            )
        except IncrementalInputError as exc:
            raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                "content_ingress_binding_mismatch",
                str(exc),
            ) from exc
        if (
            record is None
            or event.content_record_id != record.id
            or input_manifest.get("category_key") != category_key
            or runtime_context.get("content_ingress_event_db_id") != event.id
            or runtime_context.get("content_ingress_event_id") != event.event_id
            or runtime_context.get("content_ingress_payload_hash")
            != event.payload_hash
            or runtime_context.get("content_record_id") != record.id
            or runtime_context.get("upstream_intake_payload_hash") != input_hash
            or contract is None
            or contract.direction != "upstream"
            or contract.category_key != category_key
            or contract.contract_key != contract_key
            or contract_key not in connector_keys
        ):
            raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                "content_ingress_binding_mismatch",
                "增量工作流输入与冻结的内容接入事实不一致",
            )
        return
    if source_type != "upstream_intake_event" or not isinstance(source_id, int):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_binding_required",
            "含读取上游节点的增量工作流必须绑定已接纳的上游事实",
        )
    event = db.get(UpstreamIntakeEvent, source_id)
    if event is None or event.disposition not in {"accepted", "replayed"}:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_binding_required",
            "增量工作流绑定的上游事实不存在或尚不可运行",
        )
    contract = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, event.contract_version_id)
    if (
        contract is None
        or contract.direction != "upstream"
        or contract.category_key != event.category_key
        or event.category_key != category_key
    ):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_binding_mismatch",
            "增量工作流、类目与上游合同归属不一致",
        )
    connector_keys = {
        config.get("connector_key")
        for node in upstream_nodes
        if isinstance((config := node.get("config")), Mapping)
    }
    payload_json = canonical_json(dict(input_manifest))
    payload_hash = hashlib.sha256(payload_json.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    if (
        contract.contract_key not in connector_keys
        or event.payload_json != payload_json
        or event.payload_hash != payload_hash
        or runtime_context.get("upstream_intake_event_id") != event.id
        or runtime_context.get("upstream_intake_contract_version_id")
        != contract.id
        or runtime_context.get("upstream_intake_contract_key")
        != contract.contract_key
        or runtime_context.get("upstream_intake_payload_hash") != payload_hash
    ):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_binding_mismatch",
            "增量工作流输入与冻结的上游接入事实不一致",
        )
    if event.production_run_id is not None:
        existing = db.get(ProductionRun, event.production_run_id)
        if existing is None or existing.idempotency_key != idempotency_key:
            raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                "upstream_intake_run_conflict",
                "上游接入事实已经绑定其他增量运行",
            )


def create_incremental_run_from_intake(
    db: Session,
    *,
    event_id: int,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    actor: str,
) -> ProductionRun:
    """Atomically bind one accepted intake fact to one frozen workflow run."""

    event = db.scalar(
        select(UpstreamIntakeEvent)
        .where(UpstreamIntakeEvent.id == event_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if event is None:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_event_missing",
            "上游接入事实不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if event.disposition not in {"accepted", "replayed"}:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_event_not_runnable",
            "只有已接纳或明确重放的上游事实可以进入增量工作流",
        )
    if event.production_run_id is not None:
        existing = db.get(ProductionRun, event.production_run_id)
        if existing is None or existing.workflow_version_id != workflow_version_id:
            raise WorkflowConnectorError(
                "upstream_intake_run_conflict",
                "上游接入事实已经绑定其他评测机制运行",
            )
        return existing

    version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflow_version_id)
    if version is None:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "workflow_version_not_found",
            "增量评测机制版本不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    from .workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest

    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
    if (
        version.status != "active"
        or version.workflow_schema_version != "workflow-v3"
        or manifest.get("category_key") != event.category_key
        or "incremental" not in (manifest.get("run_modes") or [])
    ):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "incremental_workflow_version_invalid",
            "所选评测机制不是该类目可运行的原生增量工作流",
        )
    try:
        payload = json.loads(event.payload_json)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_payload_invalid",
            "上游接入事实内容损坏",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_payload_invalid",
            "上游接入事实内容不是对象",
        )
    if (
        hashlib.sha256(event.payload_json.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
        != event.payload_hash
    ):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_payload_hash_mismatch",
            "上游接入事实内容校验失败",
        )

    from .workflow_runtime import ProductionRunRequest, create_production_run

    source_contract = db.get(
        ConnectorContractVersion,
        event.contract_version_id,
    )
    if (
        source_contract is None
        or source_contract.direction != "upstream"
        or source_contract.category_key != event.category_key
    ):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_contract_invalid",
            "上游接入事实关联的合同版本损坏",
        )

    run = create_production_run(
        db,
        ProductionRunRequest(
            workflow_version_id=version.id,
            idempotency_key=(
                f"connector-intake:{event.contract_version_id}:"
                f"{event.source_event_id}:{event.payload_hash}"
            ),
            category_key=event.category_key,
            queue_class="production_batch",
            runtime_context={
                "run_mode": "incremental",
                "upstream_intake_event_id": event.id,
                "upstream_intake_contract_version_id": event.contract_version_id,
                "upstream_intake_contract_key": source_contract.contract_key,
                "upstream_intake_payload_hash": event.payload_hash,
                "source_event_id": event.source_event_id,
                "source_cursor": event.source_cursor,
            },
            input_manifest=payload,
            owner="connector-runtime",
            reason="已接纳上游事实进入冻结增量工作流",
            source_type="upstream_intake_event",
            source_id=event.id,
        ),
        actor=actor,
    )
    frozen = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
    workflow = frozen.get("workflow")
    frozen_manifest = (
        workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    )
    frozen_nodes = (
        frozen_manifest.get("nodes")
        if isinstance(frozen_manifest, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    upstream_contract_ids = {
        config.get("connector_snapshot", {}).get("connector_contract_id")
        for node in frozen_nodes or []
        if isinstance(node, Mapping)
        and node.get("kind") == "upstream_connector"
        and isinstance((config := node.get("config")), Mapping)
        and isinstance(config.get("connector_snapshot"), Mapping)
    }
    if event.contract_version_id not in upstream_contract_ids:
        raise WorkflowConnectorError(
            "upstream_intake_workflow_mismatch",
            "上游接入事实与工作流冻结的读取节点不一致",
        )
    event.production_run_id = run.id
    db.flush()
    return run

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_correction.py =====
"""Node-aware correction and deterministic downstream replay for workflow-v3."""

_IDENTITY_FIELDS = frozenset({"ll_id", "res_id", "source", "is_single"})
_CORRECTABLE_KINDS = frozenset({"prompt", "rule_module", "foreach_image"})

class WorkflowCorrectionError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CorrectionReplayResult:
    event_id: str
    affected_nodes: tuple[str, ...]
    normalized_result: dict[str, Any]
    result_hash: str
    truth_revision: int | None = None
    truth_hash: str | None = None
    effective_next_run: bool = False
    downstream_publish_status: str = "not_published"
    source_version: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CorrectionEvidenceGroup:
    node_instance_id: str
    asset_key: str
    component_kind: Literal["prompt", "rule_module"]
    component_version_id: int
    field_path: str
    evidence_count: int
    samples: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]
    typical_samples: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]
    evidence_hash: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WorkflowCorrectionAnalysis:
    groups: tuple[CorrectionEvidenceGroup, ...]
    blockers: tuple[str, ...]
    baseline_workflow_version_id: int
    baseline_run_id: int = 0
    category_key: str = ""
    selected_item_ids: tuple[int, ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ComponentRecommendation:
    node_instance_id: str
    asset_key: str
    source_version_id: int
    candidate_version_id: int
    mode: Literal["reused", "generated"]
    evidence_hash: str
    human_text_choices: tuple[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], ...] = ()


def _json_object(value: Any, *, code: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return deepcopy(dict(value))
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value or "{}")
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError) as exc:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(code, "冻结 JSON 内容损坏") from exc
    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(code, "冻结 JSON 内容必须是对象")
    return parsed


def _json_list(value: Any, *, code: str) -> list[Any]:
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return deepcopy(value)
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value or "[]")
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError) as exc:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(code, "冻结 JSON 内容损坏") from exc
    if not isinstance(parsed, list):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(code, "冻结 JSON 内容必须是数组")
    return parsed


def _snapshot_from_run(run: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if isinstance(run, Mapping):
        raw: Any = run
    else:
        # 存量回归运行的快照会被压缩进 workflow_snapshot_blobs（snapshot_json 置空），
        # 直接读列会把 09-24 之前的所有回归运行都判成「缺少冻结定义」。走统一读取入口。
        from .workflow_snapshot_store import SnapshotStoreError, read_run_snapshot_text

        try:
            raw = read_run_snapshot_text(run)
        except SnapshotStoreError as exc:
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_correction_snapshot_invalid", f"冻结运行快照不可读：{exc}"
            ) from exc
    snapshot = _json_object(raw, code="workflow_correction_snapshot_invalid")
    workflow = snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(manifest, Mapping) or manifest.get("schema_version") != "workflow-v3":
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_snapshot_invalid",
            "纠偏运行缺少冻结的 workflow-v3 定义",
        )
    return snapshot


def build_workflow_correction_contract(
    run: Any,
    node_results: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build an operator contract and mark non-selected route nodes read-only."""

    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(_snapshot_from_run(run))
    for node in contract.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            continue
        metadata = node.get("metadata")
        metadata = metadata if isinstance(metadata, dict) else {}
        node["metadata"] = metadata
        node_id = str(metadata.get("node_instance_id") or "")
        result = node_results.get(node_id)
        status = result.get("status") if isinstance(result, Mapping) else None
        if isinstance(status, str):
            metadata["runtime_status"] = status
        if status == "skipped":
            metadata["editable"] = False
            metadata["read_only_reason"] = str(
                result.get("skipped_reason") or "route_not_selected"
            )
    contract["contract_hash"] = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    return contract


def _manifest(snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    workflow = snapshot["workflow"]
    return dict(workflow["manifest"])


def _nodes_by_id(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    raw_nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(raw_nodes, list):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_manifest_invalid",
            "工作流节点定义损坏",
        )
    result: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for raw in raw_nodes:
        if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_correction_manifest_invalid",
                "工作流包含无效节点",
            )
        node_id = raw.get("node_instance_id")
        if not isinstance(node_id, str) or not node_id or node_id in result:
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_correction_manifest_invalid",
                "工作流节点实例标识无效或重复",
            )
        result[node_id] = deepcopy(dict(raw))
    return result


def _graph(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any], node_ids: set[str]
) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, list[str]], list[str]]:
    incoming = {node_id: [] for node_id in node_ids}
    outgoing = {node_id: [] for node_id in node_ids}
    raw_edges = manifest.get("edges")
    if not isinstance(raw_edges, list):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_manifest_invalid",
            "工作流连线定义损坏",
        )
    for edge in raw_edges:
        if not isinstance(edge, Mapping):
            continue
        source = edge.get("from")
        target = edge.get("to")
        if source not in outgoing or target not in incoming:
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_correction_manifest_invalid",
                "工作流连线引用了未知节点",
            )
        outgoing[str(source)].append(str(target))
        incoming[str(target)].append(str(source))
    indegree = {node_id: len(predecessors) for node_id, predecessors in incoming.items()}
    ready = sorted(node_id for node_id, count in indegree.items() if count == 0)
    order: list[str] = []
    while ready:
        current = ready.pop(0)
        order.append(current)
        for target in outgoing[current]:
            indegree[target] -= 1
            if indegree[target] == 0:
                ready.append(target)
                ready.sort()
    if len(order) != len(node_ids):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_manifest_invalid",
            "工作流纠偏不能重放存在环的流程",
        )
    return incoming, outgoing, order


def _latest_attempt_outputs(
    db: Session, run_id: int
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    attempts = db.scalars(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
        .order_by(
            ProductionStepAttempt.step_key,
            ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc(),
        )
    ).all()
    latest: dict[str, ProductionStepAttempt] = {}
    for attempt in attempts:
        latest.setdefault(attempt.step_key, attempt)
    outputs: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for node_id, attempt in latest.items():
        if attempt.status not in {"succeeded", "skipped"}:
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_correction_run_incomplete",
                f"节点 {node_id} 尚未形成可纠偏的终态证据",
            )
        outputs[node_id] = _json_object(
            attempt.output_manifest_json,
            code="workflow_correction_node_output_invalid",
        )
    return outputs


def _current_outputs(
    db: Session,
    run: ProductionRun,
    evaluation: EvaluationResult,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    stored = _json_object(
        evaluation.workflow_node_outputs_json,
        code="workflow_correction_node_output_invalid",
    )
    if stored:
        if not all(isinstance(value, Mapping) for value in stored.values()):
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_correction_node_output_invalid",
                "纠偏读模型中的节点输出损坏",
            )
        return {
            str(key): deepcopy(dict(value)) for key, value in stored.items()
        }
    return _latest_attempt_outputs(db, run.id)


def _path_parts(field_path: str) -> list[str]:
    normalized = field_path.strip()
    if normalized.startswith("output."):
        normalized = normalized[len("output.") :]
    if normalized in {"", "output"}:
        return []
    parts = normalized.split(".")
    if any(not part for part in parts):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_field_invalid",
            "纠偏字段路径无效",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if any(part in _IDENTITY_FIELDS for part in parts):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "route_identity_correction_forbidden",
            "路由来源、素材身份和单体标识不能在结果纠偏中改写",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return parts


def _replace_field(
    output: Mapping[str, Any],
    parts: list[str],
    value: Any,
    *,
    allow_missing_leaf: bool = False,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Any]:
    if not parts:
        if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_correction_value_invalid",
                "完整节点输出必须是对象",
                status_code=422,
            )
        return deepcopy(dict(value)), deepcopy(dict(output))
    corrected = deepcopy(dict(output))
    current: Any = corrected
    for part in parts[:-1]:
        if not isinstance(current, dict) or part not in current:
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_correction_field_missing",
                "纠偏字段不属于本次冻结节点输出",
                status_code=422,
            )
        current = current[part]
    leaf = parts[-1]
    if not isinstance(current, dict):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_field_missing",
            "纠偏字段不属于本次冻结节点输出",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if leaf not in current and not allow_missing_leaf:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_field_missing",
            "纠偏字段不属于本次冻结节点输出",
            status_code=422,
        )
    old_value = deepcopy(current.get(leaf))
    current[leaf] = deepcopy(value)
    return corrected, old_value


def _replace_foreach_item_field(
    output: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    item_key: str,
    field_path: str,
    value: Any,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Any]:
    corrected = deepcopy(dict(output))
    items = corrected.get("items")
    if not isinstance(items, list):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_foreach_output_invalid",
            "逐图节点输出缺少冻结子项列表",
            status_code=422,
        )
    selected = next(
        (
            item
            for item in items
            if isinstance(item, dict)
            and str(item.get("item_key")) == item_key
        ),
        None,
    )
    if selected is None:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_foreach_item_missing",
            "逐图纠偏子项不属于本次冻结运行",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if selected.get("status") != "succeeded" or not isinstance(
        selected.get("output"), Mapping
    ):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_node_read_only",
            "未成功执行的逐图子项只能查看，不能修改",
            status_code=422,
        )
    corrected_item, old_value = _replace_field(
        selected["output"],
        _path_parts(field_path),
        value,
    )
    selected["output"] = corrected_item
    selected["output_hash"] = hash_manifest(corrected_item)
    return corrected, old_value


def _reachable(start: str, outgoing: Mapping[str, list[str]]) -> set[str]:
    found = {start}
    pending = list(outgoing[start])
    while pending:
        current = pending.pop(0)
        if current in found:
            continue
        found.add(current)
        pending.extend(outgoing[current])
    return found


def _status(output: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return str(output.get("status") or "")


def correction_paid_prompt_descendants(
    run: ProductionRun | Mapping[str, Any],
    node_instance_id: str,
) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Return frozen downstream prompt calls that a correction would invalidate."""

    snapshot = _snapshot_from_run(run)
    manifest = _manifest(snapshot)
    nodes = _nodes_by_id(manifest)
    if node_instance_id not in nodes:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_node_not_found",
            "纠偏节点不属于本次冻结工作流",
            status_code=404,
        )
    _incoming, outgoing, _order = _graph(manifest, set(nodes))
    reachable = _reachable(node_instance_id, outgoing)
    return tuple(
        sorted(
            node_id
            for node_id in reachable
            if node_id != node_instance_id
            and nodes[node_id].get("kind") == "prompt"
        )
    )


def _paid_correction_attempt_key(
    *, run_id: int, correction_key: str, node_instance_id: str
) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(
            {
                "run_id": run_id,
                "correction_key": correction_key,
                "node_instance_id": node_instance_id,
            }
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return f"workflow-correction-paid-rerun:{digest}"


def _execute_paid_correction_prompt(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run: ProductionRun,
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    correction_key: str,
    model_client_type: Callable[[Any], Any] | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Run one invalidated prompt with a durable, idempotent paid-call ledger."""

    node_id = str(node.get("node_instance_id") or "")
    executable = prompt_execution_node(node)
    if executable is None or not node_id:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_paid_node_invalid",
            "纠偏重跑目标不是可执行提示词节点",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if run.model_execution_mode != "real":
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_paid_rerun_real_run_required",
            "只有真实模型运行可以在纠偏后发起下游付费重跑",
            status_code=409,
        )
    input_hash = hash_manifest(input_manifest)
    attempt_key = _paid_correction_attempt_key(
        run_id=run.id,
        correction_key=correction_key,
        node_instance_id=node_id,
    )
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.idempotency_key == attempt_key
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        if (
            existing.run_id != run.id
            or existing.step_key != node_id
            or existing.input_hash != input_hash
        ):
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_correction_paid_rerun_idempotency_conflict",
                "相同纠偏费用确认已绑定不同的下游提示词输入",
                status_code=409,
            )
        if existing.status == "succeeded":
            replayed = _json_object(
                existing.output_manifest_json,
                code="workflow_correction_paid_rerun_result_invalid",
            )
            if replayed.get("status") != "succeeded":
                raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                    "workflow_correction_paid_rerun_result_invalid",
                    "已结算的纠偏重跑结果损坏",
                )
            return replayed
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_paid_rerun_incomplete",
            "相同纠偏的真实模型重跑已发起但没有成功终态；为避免重复计费已拒绝再次调用",
            status_code=409,
        )

    base_attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
            ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == node_id,
        )
        .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc())
    )
    if base_attempt is None:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_correction_paid_rerun_attempt_missing",
            "纠偏重跑找不到原始提示词步骤",
            status_code=409,
        )
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
        run_id=run.id,
        step_key=node_id,
        step_type=base_attempt.step_type,
        sequence=base_attempt.sequence,
        script_version_id=base_attempt.script_version_id,
        status="leased",
        attempt_no=base_attempt.attempt_no + 1,
        idempotency_key=attempt_key,
        input_manifest_json=canonical_json(dict(input_manifest)),
        input_hash=input_hash,
        output_manifest_json="{}",
        checkpoint_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "schema_version": "workflow-correction-paid-rerun-v1",
                "correction_key": correction_key,
                "real_execution_confirmed": True,
            }
        ),
        last_error_code="",
        last_error_message="",
        lease_owner="workflow-correction",
        lease_token=hashlib.sha256(attempt_key.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:64],
        lease_expires_at=now + timedelta(minutes=30),
        heartbeat_at=now,
        started_at=now,
    )
    db.add(attempt)
    db.flush()
    try:
        prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
            db,
            run=run,
            attempt=attempt,
            node=executable,
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
        )
        from .batch_submission import estimate_prepared_submission
        invocation = reserve_model_invocation(
            db,
            attempt_id=attempt.id,
            node=executable,
            run_snapshot=snapshot,
            reserved_images=len(prepared.media_dispatch_identities),
            dispatch_identity=prepared.dispatch_identity,
            paid_correction_confirmed=True,
            endpoint_id=prepared.endpoint_id,
            submission_estimate=estimate_prepared_submission(prepared),
        )
        if prepared.media_bundle.items:
            record_prepared_media(db, invocation.id, prepared.media_bundle)
        invocation_id = invocation.id
        attempt_id = attempt.id
        db.commit()
    except (WorkflowModelExecutorError, WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc:
        db.rollback()
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            getattr(exc, "code", "workflow_correction_paid_rerun_prepare_failed"),
            str(exc),
            status_code=getattr(exc, "status_code", 409),
        ) from exc

    provider_dispatched = False

    def mark_provider_dispatched() -> None:
        nonlocal provider_dispatched
        provider_dispatched = True

    try:
        real_result = asyncio.run(
            execute_prepared_prompt(
                prepared,
                client_type=model_client_type or DoubaoClient,
                on_provider_dispatch=mark_provider_dispatched,
            )
        )
        provider_dispatched = True
    except Exception as exc:
        try:
            invocation = db.get(type(invocation), invocation_id)
            if invocation is not None and invocation.status == "reserved":
                fail_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    invocation_id=invocation_id,
                    error_code=getattr(exc, "code", "model_provider_error"),
                    error_message=str(exc),
                    request_correlation_id=getattr(
                        exc, "request_correlation_id", None
                    ),
                    upstream_status_code=getattr(exc, "upstream_status_code", None),
                    # 连接池排队超时声明请求未发出，覆盖提前触发的 dispatch hook。
                    provider_dispatched=(
                        provider_dispatched
                        and getattr(exc, "provider_dispatched", True) is not False
                    ),
                )
            failed_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
            if failed_attempt is not None:
                failed_attempt.status = "failed"
                failed_attempt.last_error_code = str(
                    getattr(exc, "code", "model_provider_error")
                )[:80]
                failed_attempt.last_error_message = str(exc)
                failed_attempt.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
                failed_attempt.lease_owner = None
                failed_attempt.lease_token = None
                failed_attempt.lease_expires_at = None
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            getattr(exc, "code", "workflow_correction_paid_rerun_failed"),
            str(exc),
            status_code=409,
        ) from exc

    settlement_committed = False
    try:
        if prepared.media_bundle.items:
            media_pricing_snapshot = dict(prepared.media_pricing_snapshot)
            if (
                media_pricing_snapshot.get("version")
                in MODEL_PRICING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS
            ):
                media_pricing_snapshot["request_input_tokens"] = (
                    getattr(real_result, "media_request_input_tokens", None)
