            if canonical_component_hash(_prompt_content(prompt)) == digest
        ),
        None,
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    binding_count = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.id)).where(
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id.is_not(None),
        )
    )
    candidate = PromptVersion(
        category_key=asset.category_key,
        pipeline_scope=source.pipeline_scope,
        stage=source.stage,
        name=asset.display_name,
        version=f"component-v{int(binding_count or 0) + 1}",
        system_prompt=content["system_prompt"],
        user_prompt=content["user_prompt"],
        rubric_version=content["rubric_version"],
        status="published",
        source="automatic_correction",
        change_note=(
            "根据人工纠偏证据自动生成候选；"
            f"源版本 {source.version}；证据 {evidence_fingerprint[:16]}"
        ),
        created_by=created_by,
    )
    db.add(candidate)
    db.flush()
    try:
        bind_prompt_version(
            db,
            asset=asset,
            prompt_version=candidate,
            created_by=created_by,
        )
    except WorkflowAssetError as exc:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_generation_unavailable",
            str(exc),
            status_code=exc.status_code,
        ) from exc
    return candidate


def _generate_rule_candidate_from_evidence(
    db: Session,
    *,
    asset: WorkflowNodeAsset,
    source_version_id: int,
    groups: Sequence[CorrectionEvidenceGroup],
    created_by: str,
) -> RuleModuleVersion:
    """Create one immutable rule candidate without changing its working copy."""

    from .evaluation_component_catalog import (
        canonical_component_hash,
        validate_module_content,
    )
    from .workflow_assets import WorkflowAssetError, bind_rule_module_version

    source = db.get(RuleModuleVersion, source_version_id)
    if (
        source is None
        or source.asset_id != asset.id
        or source.module_type_key != asset.module_type_key
    ):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_source_missing",
            "规则模块节点绑定的源版本不存在或不属于当前节点",
        )
    evidence_fingerprint = hash_manifest(
        {
            "schema_version": "rule-correction-guidance-v1",
            "asset_key": asset.asset_key,
            "source_version_id": source_version_id,
            "groups": [
                {
                    "field_path": group.field_path,
                    "evidence_hash": group.evidence_hash,
                }
                for group in sorted(groups, key=lambda item: item.field_path)
            ],
        }
    )
    fields: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for group in sorted(groups, key=lambda item: item.field_path):
        reasons = sorted(
            {
                " ".join(str(sample.get("reason") or "").split())[:500]
                for sample in group.samples
                if str(sample.get("reason") or "").strip()
            }
        )
        fields.append(
            {
                "field_path": group.field_path,
                "operator_guidance": reasons
                or ["仅在规则证据充分时给出结论"],
            }
        )
    content = _json_object(
        source.content_json,
        code="workflow_candidate_rule_content_invalid",
    )
    content["correction_guidance"] = {
        "schema_version": "rule-correction-guidance-v1",
        "source_version_id": source_version_id,
        "evidence_fingerprint": evidence_fingerprint,
        "fields": fields,
    }
    normalized = validate_module_content(source.module_type_key, content)
    digest = canonical_component_hash(normalized)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(RuleModuleVersion).where(
            RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id,
            RuleModuleVersion.content_hash == digest,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    maximum = db.scalar(
        select(func.max(RuleModuleVersion.version)).where(
            RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id
        )
    )
    candidate = RuleModuleVersion(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        version=int(maximum or 0) + 1,
        module_type_key=source.module_type_key,
        content_json=canonical_json(normalized),
        content_hash=digest,
        source_working_copy_revision=source.source_working_copy_revision,
        created_by=created_by,
    )
    db.add(candidate)
    db.flush()
    try:
        bind_rule_module_version(
            db,
            asset=asset,
            rule_module_version=candidate,
            created_by=created_by,
        )
    except WorkflowAssetError as exc:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_generation_unavailable",
            str(exc),
            status_code=exc.status_code,
        ) from exc
    return candidate


def recommend_component_versions(
    db: Session,
    analysis: WorkflowCorrectionAnalysis,
) -> list[ComponentRecommendation]:
    """Reuse a proven version, otherwise generate one from edits or evidence."""

    if analysis.blockers:
        messages = {"human_truth_route_requires_regression": "路线已修改，人工真值已保存；请按新路线回归后再分析模型表现"}
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_evidence_blocked",
            "人工证据尚不能安全生成候选：" + "、".join(messages.get(code, code) for code in analysis.blockers),
            status_code=422,
        )
    if not analysis.groups:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_evidence_missing",
            "没有可用于组件推荐的人工节点证据",
            status_code=422,
        )
    by_node: dict[
        tuple[str, str, Literal["prompt", "rule_module"], int],
        list[CorrectionEvidenceGroup],
    ] = {}
    for group in analysis.groups:
        by_node.setdefault(
            (
                group.node_instance_id,
                group.asset_key,
                group.component_kind,
                group.component_version_id,
            ),
            [],
        ).append(group)
    recommendations: list[ComponentRecommendation] = []
    for (node_id, asset_key, kind, source_id), groups in sorted(by_node.items()):
        asset = db.scalar(
            select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == analysis.category_key,
                WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == asset_key,
                WorkflowNodeAsset.kind == kind,
            )
        )
        if asset is None:
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_candidate_asset_missing",
                f"节点 {node_id} 的组件资产不存在",
            )
        candidate_id = next(
            (
                version_id
                for version_id in _component_candidates(db, asset=asset, kind=kind)
                if version_id != source_id
                and _candidate_version_passes(
                    db,
                    analysis=analysis,
                    groups=groups,
                    candidate_version_id=version_id,
                )
            ),
            None,
        )
        mode: Literal["reused", "generated"]
        if candidate_id is not None:
            mode = "reused"
        else:
            from .evaluation_components import (
                EvaluationComponentError,
                generate_component_version,
            )

            try:
                if kind == "prompt" and _prompt_needs_evidence_generation(
                    db,
                    asset=asset,
                    source_version_id=source_id,
                ):
                    generated_id = _generate_prompt_candidate_from_evidence(
                        db,
                        asset=asset,
                        source_version_id=source_id,
                        groups=groups,
                        created_by="automatic-correction",
                    ).id
                elif kind == "rule_module" and _working_rule_matches_source(
                    db,
                    asset=asset,
                    source_version_id=source_id,
                ):
                    generated_id = _generate_rule_candidate_from_evidence(
                        db,
                        asset=asset,
                        source_version_id=source_id,
                        groups=groups,
                        created_by="automatic-correction",
                    ).id
                else:
                    generated_id = generate_component_version(
                        db,
                        asset_id=asset.id,
                        created_by="automatic-correction",
                    ).id
            except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
                raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                    "workflow_candidate_generation_unavailable",
                    str(exc),
                    status_code=exc.status_code,
                ) from exc
            candidate_id = generated_id
            mode = "generated"
        if candidate_id == source_id:
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_candidate_component_unchanged",
                f"节点 {node_id} 的工作副本尚未形成不同版本",
                status_code=422,
            )
        evidence_hash = hash_manifest(
            {
                "schema_version": "workflow-component-recommendation-evidence-v1",
                "node_instance_id": node_id,
                "asset_key": asset_key,
                "source_version_id": source_id,
                "groups": [group.evidence_hash for group in groups],
            }
        )
        recommendations.append(
            ComponentRecommendation(
                node_instance_id=node_id,
                asset_key=asset_key,
                source_version_id=source_id,
                candidate_version_id=int(candidate_id),
                mode=mode,
                evidence_hash=evidence_hash,
                human_text_choices=tuple(
                    (group.field_path, tuple(dict.fromkeys(
                        value
                        for sample in group.samples
                        for value in (sample.get("new_value") if isinstance(sample.get("new_value"), list) else [sample.get("new_value")])
                        if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip() and len(value) <= 2000
                    )))
                    for group in groups if kind == "prompt"
                ),
            )
        )
    return recommendations


def _prompt_component_snapshot(prompt: PromptVersion) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from .evaluation_component_catalog import canonical_component_hash

    content = {
        "stage": prompt.stage,
        "system_prompt": prompt.system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": prompt.user_prompt,
        "rubric_version": prompt.rubric_version,
    }
    return {
        "kind": "prompt",
        "version_id": prompt.id,
        "version": prompt.version,
        "content": content,
        "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(content),
    }


def _candidate_component(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    recommendation: ComponentRecommendation,
) -> tuple[
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    str,
    dict[str, Any],
    Literal["prompt", "rule_module"],
]:
    binding = _candidate_component_config(node)
    if binding is None:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_component_kind_invalid",
            "当前节点不拥有可版本化的提示词或规则组件",
            status_code=422,
        )
    kind, config = binding
    version_field = _component_version_field(kind)
    if (
        config.get("asset_key") != recommendation.asset_key
        or config.get(version_field) != recommendation.source_version_id
    ):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_source_drift",
            f"节点 {recommendation.node_instance_id} 的冻结组件已变化",
        )
    asset = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == recommendation.asset_key,
        )
    )
    if asset is None or asset.kind != kind:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_asset_missing",
            f"节点 {recommendation.node_instance_id} 的组件资产不存在",
        )
    if kind == "prompt":
        prompt = db.get(PromptVersion, recommendation.candidate_version_id)
        bound = (
            db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.id).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id
                    == recommendation.candidate_version_id,
                )
            )
            is not None
        )
        if (
            prompt is None
            or prompt.category_key != category_key
            or not bound
        ):
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_candidate_category_mismatch",
                "候选提示词必须来自当前类目的同一节点资产；跨类目请先复制",
                status_code=422,
            )
        return asset, version_field, _prompt_component_snapshot(prompt), kind
    rule = db.get(RuleModuleVersion, recommendation.candidate_version_id)
    if (
        rule is None
        or rule.asset_id != asset.id
        or rule.module_type_key != asset.module_type_key
    ):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_category_mismatch",
            "候选规则必须来自当前类目的同一模块资产；跨类目请先复制",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return (
        asset,
        version_field,
        {
            "kind": "rule_module",
            "version_id": rule.id,
            "version": str(rule.version),
            "module_type_key": rule.module_type_key,
            "content_json": rule.content_json,
            "content_hash": rule.content_hash,
        },
        kind,
    )


def compose_candidate_workflow(
    db: Session,
    baseline_workflow_version_id: int,
    recommendations: Sequence[ComponentRecommendation],
    correction_run_id: int,
    actor: str,
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    """Persist an immutable candidate with evidence-backed textual enum additions."""

    recommendation_snapshot = sorted(
        (
            {
                "node_instance_id": item.node_instance_id,
                "asset_key": item.asset_key,
                "source_version_id": item.source_version_id,
                "candidate_version_id": item.candidate_version_id,
                "mode": item.mode,
                "evidence_hash": item.evidence_hash,
                **({"human_text_choices": [[path, list(values)] for path, values in item.human_text_choices]}
                   if item.human_text_choices else {}),
            }
            for item in recommendations
        ),
        key=lambda item: (
            item["node_instance_id"],
            item["asset_key"],
            item["source_version_id"],
            item["candidate_version_id"],
        ),
    )

    correction = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_run_id)
    if correction is None:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_correction_not_found",
            "纠偏任务不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if correction.candidate_workflow_version_id is not None:
        existing = db.get(
            WorkflowVersion, correction.candidate_workflow_version_id
        )
        if existing is None:
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_candidate_binding_invalid",
                "纠偏任务绑定的候选工作流已损坏",
            )
        orchestration = _json_object(
            correction.orchestration_json,
            code="workflow_candidate_orchestration_invalid",
        )
        frozen_candidate = orchestration.get("native_candidate")
        if not isinstance(frozen_candidate, Mapping):
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_candidate_binding_invalid",
                "纠偏任务缺少候选工作流的冻结编排证据",
            )
        frozen_recommendations = frozen_candidate.get("recommendations")
        normalized_frozen = (
            sorted(
                (dict(item) for item in frozen_recommendations),
                key=lambda item: (
                    item.get("node_instance_id"),
                    item.get("asset_key"),
                    item.get("source_version_id"),
                    item.get("candidate_version_id"),
                ),
            )
            if isinstance(frozen_recommendations, list)
            and all(isinstance(item, Mapping) for item in frozen_recommendations)
            else None
        )
        if (
            frozen_candidate.get("baseline_workflow_version_id")
            != baseline_workflow_version_id
            or frozen_candidate.get("candidate_workflow_version_id") != existing.id
            or normalized_frozen != recommendation_snapshot
        ):
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_candidate_idempotency_conflict",
                "纠偏任务已绑定另一组候选组件，不能覆盖冻结候选",
            )
        return existing
    baseline = db.get(WorkflowVersion, baseline_workflow_version_id)
    if baseline is None or baseline.workflow_schema_version != "workflow-v3":
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_baseline_invalid",
            "候选必须从冻结的 workflow-v3 版本生成",
        )
    if correction.baseline_run.workflow_version_id != baseline.id:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_baseline_mismatch",
            "纠偏任务与候选基线工作流不一致",
        )
    if not recommendations:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_recommendations_empty",
            "没有可写入候选工作流的组件推荐",
            status_code=422,
        )
    from .workflow_node_executors import (
        NodeExecutorError,
        assert_native_workflow_executable,
    )
    from .workflow_registry import (
        WorkflowRegistryError,
        create_workflow_version,
        persisted_workflow_manifest,
    )

    manifest = deepcopy(persisted_workflow_manifest(baseline))
    baseline_prompt_contract_identities = list(
        prompt_contract_identities_from_snapshot(manifest)
    )
    raw_nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(raw_nodes, list):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_manifest_invalid",
            "候选基线缺少节点定义",
        )
    nodes = {
        str(node["node_instance_id"]): node
        for node in raw_nodes
        if isinstance(node, dict)
        and isinstance(node.get("node_instance_id"), str)
    }
    seen_nodes: set[str] = set()
    details: list[
        tuple[
            ComponentRecommendation,
            WorkflowNodeAsset,
            Literal["prompt", "rule_module"],
            dict[str, Any],
        ]
    ] = []
    for recommendation in recommendations:
        if recommendation.node_instance_id in seen_nodes:
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_candidate_node_duplicate",
                "同一节点只能有一个组件推荐",
                status_code=422,
            )
        seen_nodes.add(recommendation.node_instance_id)
        node = nodes.get(recommendation.node_instance_id)
        if node is None:
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_candidate_node_missing",
                f"候选节点不存在：{recommendation.node_instance_id}",
            )
        asset, version_field, snapshot, component_kind = _candidate_component(
            db,
            category_key=str(manifest.get("category_key") or ""),
            node=node,
            recommendation=recommendation,
        )
        candidate_binding = _candidate_component_config(node)
        assert candidate_binding is not None
        node_config = candidate_binding[1]
        assert isinstance(node_config, dict)
        node_config[version_field] = recommendation.candidate_version_id
        node_config["component_snapshot"] = snapshot
        details.append((recommendation, asset, component_kind, snapshot))
    candidate_prompt_contract_identities = list(
        prompt_contract_identities_from_snapshot(manifest)
    )
    if candidate_prompt_contract_identities != baseline_prompt_contract_identities:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_prompt_contract_drift",
            "组件候选不能隐式改写冻结输出合同或视觉策略",
            status_code=422,
        )
    # Component replacement must preserve contracts (checked above). Only now
    # add human-backed text choices to both copies in this NEW workflow.
    enum_extensions: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for recommendation in recommendations:
        node = nodes[recommendation.node_instance_id]
        for field_path, values in recommendation.human_text_choices:
            before = deepcopy(node)
            for value in values:
                node.update(_human_validation_node(node, field_path, value))
                # A list field's enum lives in items, while a scalar's is direct.
                node.update(_human_validation_node(node, field_path, [value]))
            changes = []
            for location, old_schema, new_schema in (
                ("output_schema", before.get("output_schema"), node.get("output_schema")),
                ("output_contract.schema", (before.get("config") or {}).get("output_contract", {}).get("schema"),
                 (node.get("config") or {}).get("output_contract", {}).get("schema")),
            ):
                for part in _path_parts(field_path):
                    old_schema = old_schema.get("properties", {}).get(part) if isinstance(old_schema, Mapping) else None
                    new_schema = new_schema.get("properties", {}).get(part) if isinstance(new_schema, Mapping) else None
                if isinstance(old_schema, dict) and isinstance(new_schema, dict):
                    if old_schema.get("type") == "array":
                        old_schema, new_schema = old_schema.get("items", {}), new_schema.get("items", {})
                    added = [v for v in new_schema.get("enum", []) if v not in old_schema.get("enum", [])]
                    if added:
                        changes.append({"schema": location, "added_values": added})
            if changes:
                enum_extensions.append({"node_instance_id": recommendation.node_instance_id,
                    "field_path": field_path, "evidence_hash": recommendation.evidence_hash,
                    "changes": changes})
    candidate_prompt_contract_identities = list(prompt_contract_identities_from_snapshot(manifest))
    try:
        assert_native_workflow_executable(manifest)
        version_name = f"candidate-correction-{correction.id}"
        row = create_workflow_version(
            db,
            definition=baseline.definition,
            version=version_name,
            manifest=manifest,
            created_by=actor,
        )
    except (WorkflowRegistryError, NodeExecutorError) as exc:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            getattr(exc, "code", "workflow_candidate_manifest_invalid"),
            str(exc),
            status_code=getattr(exc, "status_code", 422),
        ) from exc
    row.status = "active"
    row.validation_report_json = canonical_json({"ok": True, "errors": []})
    correction.candidate_workflow_version_id = row.id
    correction.stage = "candidate_validation"
    correction.progress = max(correction.progress, 60)
    for recommendation, asset, kind, snapshot in details:
        detail = BaselineCorrectionComponentCandidate(
            correction_run_id=correction.id,
            node_instance_id=recommendation.node_instance_id,
            asset_id=asset.id,
            source_prompt_version_id=(
                recommendation.source_version_id if kind == "prompt" else None
            ),
            candidate_prompt_version_id=(
                recommendation.candidate_version_id if kind == "prompt" else None
            ),
            source_rule_module_version_id=(
                recommendation.source_version_id
                if kind == "rule_module"
                else None
            ),
            candidate_rule_module_version_id=(
                recommendation.candidate_version_id
                if kind == "rule_module"
                else None
            ),
            selection_kind=recommendation.mode,
            evidence_summary_json=canonical_json(
                {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-component-recommendation-v1",
                    "asset_key": recommendation.asset_key,
                    "evidence_hash": recommendation.evidence_hash,
                    "enum_extensions": [change for change in enum_extensions
                                        if change["node_instance_id"] == recommendation.node_instance_id],
                }
            ),
            content_hash=str(snapshot["content_hash"]),
            created_by=actor,
        )
        db.add(detail)
    orchestration = _json_object(
        correction.orchestration_json,
        code="workflow_candidate_orchestration_invalid",
    )
    orchestration["native_candidate"] = {
        "schema_version": "native-workflow-candidate-v1",
        "baseline_workflow_version_id": baseline.id,
        "candidate_workflow_version_id": row.id,
        "prompt_contract_identities": candidate_prompt_contract_identities,
        "baseline_prompt_contract_identities": baseline_prompt_contract_identities,
        "enum_extensions": enum_extensions,
        "recommendations": recommendation_snapshot,
    }
    correction.orchestration_json = canonical_json(orchestration)
    db.flush()
    return row


def start_single_candidate_regression(
    db: Session,
    correction_run: BaselineCorrectionRun,
    actor: str,
) -> BaselineRegressionRun:
    """Create or return the correction round's sole native candidate run."""

    if correction_run.regression_run_id is not None:
        existing = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, correction_run.regression_run_id)
        if existing is None:
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_candidate_regression_binding_invalid",
                "纠偏任务绑定的候选回归已损坏",
            )
        return existing
    source = correction_run.baseline_run
    if source.execution_engine != "native_workflow":
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_native_required",
            "只有原生工作流回归可以启动节点化候选回归",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if correction_run.candidate_workflow_version_id is None:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_workflow_missing",
            "候选工作流尚未生成",
        )
    candidate = db.get(
        WorkflowVersion, correction_run.candidate_workflow_version_id
    )
    if candidate is None:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_workflow_missing",
            "候选工作流绑定已损坏",
        )
    source_execution = _json_object(
        source.execution_snapshot_json,
        code="workflow_candidate_source_execution_invalid",
    )
    source_model_execution_mode = source_execution.get(
        "model_execution_mode", "simulated"
    )
    if source_model_execution_mode not in {"simulated", "real"}:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_source_execution_invalid",
            "源回归的模型执行方式已损坏",
        )
    frozen_correction = _json_object(
        correction_run.input_snapshot_json,
        code="workflow_candidate_execution_invalid",
    )
    candidate_execution = frozen_correction.get("candidate_execution")
    if candidate_execution is None and source_model_execution_mode == "simulated":
        # Historical simulated corrections predate the explicit execution
        # contract. Keeping their fixture reruns working is safe and preserves
        # backwards compatibility without granting any paid execution.
        candidate_execution = {
            "model_execution_mode": "simulated",
            "real_execution_confirmed": False,
        }
    if not isinstance(candidate_execution, Mapping):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_execution_invalid",
            "候选回归的模型执行合同已损坏",
        )
    candidate_model_execution_mode = candidate_execution.get(
        "model_execution_mode"
    )
    if candidate_model_execution_mode != source_model_execution_mode:
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_execution_mode_mismatch",
            "候选回归必须继承源回归的模型执行方式",
        )
    real_execution_confirmed = candidate_execution.get(
        "real_execution_confirmed"
    ) is True
    correction_orchestration = _json_object(
        correction_run.orchestration_json,
        code="workflow_candidate_orchestration_invalid",
    )
    retry_execution = correction_orchestration.get("retry_execution")
    if (
        isinstance(retry_execution, Mapping)
        and retry_execution.get("attempt_count") == correction_run.attempt_count
    ):
        if (
            retry_execution.get("schema_version")
            != "baseline-correction-retry-execution-v1"
            or retry_execution.get("model_execution_mode")
            != candidate_model_execution_mode
        ):
            raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
                "workflow_candidate_execution_invalid",
                "纠偏重试的模型执行确认已损坏",
            )
        real_execution_confirmed = (
            retry_execution.get("real_execution_confirmed") is True
        )
    if (
        candidate_model_execution_mode == "real"
        and not real_execution_confirmed
    ):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_real_confirmation_required",
            "真实模型候选回归需要重新确认费用和素材外发",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if (
        candidate_model_execution_mode == "simulated"
        and real_execution_confirmed
    ):
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            "workflow_candidate_confirmation_not_allowed",
            "快速模拟候选回归不能携带真实模型费用确认",
            status_code=422,
        )
    selected = [item.baseline_set_item for item in source.items]
    try:
        run = create_native_baseline_run(
            db,
            baseline_set=source.baseline_set,
            workflow_version=candidate,
            actor=actor,
            idempotency_key=(
                f"baseline-correction:{correction_run.id}:candidate:{candidate.id}"
            ),
            items=selected,
            model_execution_mode=candidate_model_execution_mode,
            real_execution_confirmed=real_execution_confirmed,
            inference_mode=source_execution.get("inference_mode", "auto"),
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        if isinstance(exc, WorkflowCorrectionError):
            raise
        raise WorkflowCorrectionError(
            getattr(exc, "code", "workflow_candidate_regression_failed"),
            str(exc),
            status_code=getattr(exc, "status_code", 409),
        ) from exc
    run.previous_run_id = source.id
    correction_run.regression_run_id = run.id
    correction_run.stage = "regression"
    correction_run.progress = max(correction_run.progress, 75)
    orchestration = correction_orchestration
    orchestration["regression"] = {
        "schema_version": "native-workflow-candidate-regression-v1",
        "run_id": run.id,
        "source_run_id": source.id,
        "candidate_workflow_version_id": candidate.id,
        "baseline_set_item_ids": [item.id for item in selected],
        "model_execution_mode": candidate_model_execution_mode,
        "real_execution_budget": _json_object(
            run.execution_snapshot_json,
            code="workflow_candidate_execution_invalid",
        ).get("real_execution_budget"),
    }
    correction_run.orchestration_json = canonical_json(orchestration)
    db.flush()
    return run

===== FILE backend/app/workflow_external_wait.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import or_, select
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .database import reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction
from .models import (
    ConnectorContractVersion,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeDispatchItem,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    ScriptVersion,
    UpstreamIntakeEvent,
    WorkflowExternalWait,
)


class ExternalWaitError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409):
        super().__init__(f"{code}: {message}")
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExternalWaitRequest:
    production_run_id: int
    request_node_instance_id: str
    resume_node_instance_id: str
    continuation_key: str
    outbox_event_id: int
    expected_contract_version_id: int
    correlation_id: str
    deadline_at: datetime
    timeout_policy: str
    audit_actor: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExternalResumeRequest:
    correlation_id: str
    callback_intake_event_id: int
    callback_payload_hash: str
    actor: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExternalWaitExpirySummary:
    expired_count: int
    manual_review_count: int
    failed_count: int
    wait_ids: tuple[int, ...]


def _now(value: datetime | None = None) -> datetime:
    current = value or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    return current if current.tzinfo is not None else current.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _required(value: str, *, name: str, maximum: int) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip()
    if not normalized:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_request_invalid",
            f"{name}不能为空",
            status_code=400,
        )
    if len(normalized) > maximum:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_request_invalid",
            f"{name}超过长度上限",
            status_code=400,
        )
    return normalized


def _hash_shape(value: str) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip().lower()
    if len(normalized) != 64 or any(
        character not in "0123456789abcdef" for character in normalized
    ):
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_callback_hash_invalid",
            "回调内容摘要格式无效",
            status_code=400,
        )
    return normalized


def _same_wait_identity(
    wait: WorkflowExternalWait,
    request: ExternalWaitRequest,
) -> bool:
    return (
        wait.production_run_id == request.production_run_id
        and wait.request_node_instance_id == request.request_node_instance_id
        and wait.resume_node_instance_id == request.resume_node_instance_id
        and wait.continuation_key == request.continuation_key
        and wait.outbox_event_id == request.outbox_event_id
        and wait.expected_contract_version_id
        == request.expected_contract_version_id
        and wait.correlation_id == request.correlation_id
        and wait.timeout_policy == request.timeout_policy
        and _now(wait.deadline_at) == _now(request.deadline_at)
        and wait.audit_actor == request.audit_actor
    )


def _existing_wait(
    db: Session,
    request: ExternalWaitRequest,
) -> WorkflowExternalWait | None:
    matches = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowExternalWait)
        .where(
            or_(
                WorkflowExternalWait.correlation_id == request.correlation_id,
                WorkflowExternalWait.outbox_event_id == request.outbox_event_id,
                (
                    WorkflowExternalWait.production_run_id
                    == request.production_run_id
                )
                & (
                    WorkflowExternalWait.continuation_key
                    == request.continuation_key
                ),
            )
        )
        .with_for_update()
    ).all()
    if not matches:
        return None
    if len(matches) != 1 or not _same_wait_identity(matches[0], request):
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_idempotency_conflict",
            "等待关联标识已经绑定其他节点、合同或截止时间",
        )
    return matches[0]


def create_external_wait(
    db: Session,
    request: ExternalWaitRequest,
) -> WorkflowExternalWait:
    if request.production_run_id < 1 or request.outbox_event_id < 1:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_request_invalid",
            "运行与 Outbox 标识必须是正整数",
            status_code=400,
        )
    if request.expected_contract_version_id < 1:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_request_invalid",
            "回调合同版本必须是正整数",
            status_code=400,
        )
    request_node = _required(
        request.request_node_instance_id,
        name="发起等待节点",
        maximum=160,
    )
    resume_node = _required(
        request.resume_node_instance_id,
        name="接收结果节点",
        maximum=160,
    )
    continuation_key = _required(
        request.continuation_key,
        name="外部续跑标识",
        maximum=160,
    )
    correlation_id = _required(
        request.correlation_id,
        name="回调关联号",
        maximum=200,
    )
    actor = _required(request.audit_actor, name="审计操作者", maximum=80)
    if request.timeout_policy not in {"manual_review", "fail"}:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_timeout_policy_invalid",
            "等待超时策略不受支持",
            status_code=400,
        )
    deadline = _now(request.deadline_at)
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == request.production_run_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if run is None:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_run_missing",
            "等待关联的运行不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    existing = _existing_wait(db, request)
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    if run.status not in {"queued", "running"}:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_run_state_invalid",
            "只有执行中的运行可以进入等待外部系统状态",
        )
    outbox = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
        .where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id == request.outbox_event_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if (
        outbox is None
        or outbox.mechanism_version_id != run.workflow_version_id
        or outbox.node_instance_id != request_node
    ):
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_outbox_mismatch",
            "等待事实与当前节点的 Outbox 事件不一致",
        )
    contract = db.get(
        ConnectorContractVersion,
        request.expected_contract_version_id,
    )
    if (
        contract is None
        or contract.direction != "upstream"
        or contract.category_key != run.category_key
    ):
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_contract_mismatch",
            "等待事实的回调合同与运行类目不一致",
        )
    wait = WorkflowExternalWait(
        production_run_id=run.id,
        request_node_instance_id=request_node,
        resume_node_instance_id=resume_node,
        continuation_key=continuation_key,
        outbox_event_id=outbox.id,
        expected_contract_version_id=contract.id,
        correlation_id=correlation_id,
        state="waiting",
        deadline_at=deadline,
        timeout_policy=request.timeout_policy,
        audit_actor=actor,
    )
    try:
        with db.begin_nested():
            db.add(wait)
            db.flush()
    except IntegrityError:
        existing = _existing_wait(db, request)
        if existing is None:
            raise
        wait = existing
    run.status = "waiting_external"
    run.current_step_key = resume_node
    run.lease_owner = None
    run.lease_token = None
    run.lease_expires_at = None
    run.heartbeat_at = None
    run.error_code = ""
    run.error_message = ""
    db.flush()
    return wait


def resolve_external_wait_contract(
    db: Session,
    correlation_id: str,
) -> ConnectorContractVersion:
    normalized = _required(
        correlation_id,
        name="回调关联号",
        maximum=200,
    )
    wait = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowExternalWait).where(
            WorkflowExternalWait.correlation_id == normalized
        )
    )
    if wait is None:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_not_found",
            "没有找到对应的外部等待事实",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if wait.state not in {"waiting", "resumed"}:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_state_invalid",
            "当前等待已经结束，不能接收回调",
        )
    contract = db.get(
        ConnectorContractVersion,
        wait.expected_contract_version_id,
    )
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, wait.production_run_id)
    if (
        contract is None
        or run is None
        or contract.direction != "upstream"
        or contract.category_key != run.category_key
    ):
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_contract_mismatch",
            "等待事实的回调合同与运行类目不一致",
        )
    return contract


def _callback_payload(event: UpstreamIntakeEvent) -> dict:
    try:
        payload = json.loads(event.payload_json)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_callback_payload_invalid",
            "回调事实内容损坏",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_callback_payload_invalid",
            "回调事实内容必须是对象",
        )
    canonical = canonical_json(payload)
    if hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() != event.payload_hash:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_callback_hash_mismatch",
            "回调事实内容与摘要不一致",
        )
    return payload


def _schedule_same_node_callback_attempt(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run: ProductionRun,
    wait: WorkflowExternalWait,
    callback_payload: dict,
    callback_payload_hash: str,
    now: datetime,
) -> ProductionStepAttempt | None:
    """Create the callback-consuming attempt for a self-resuming node.

    Ordinary external continuations resume a distinct upstream connector and
    let the DAG scheduler populate that node.  Image first-seen lookup is a
    single logical node with two phases, so its immutable callback fact must be
    consumed by a fresh attempt of the same node instead.
    """

    if wait.request_node_instance_id != wait.resume_node_instance_id:
        return None
    previous = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
            ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == wait.request_node_instance_id,
        )
        .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc())
        .limit(1)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if (
        previous is None
        or previous.status != "retryable"
        or previous.last_error_code != "external_wait_requested"
    ):
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_resume_attempt_invalid",
            "等待回调的节点尝试状态不完整，拒绝生成重复续跑任务",
        )
    script = db.get(ScriptVersion, previous.script_version_id)
    if script is None:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_resume_script_missing",
            "等待回调的节点执行合同不存在",
        )
    next_number = previous.attempt_no + 1
    next_attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
        run_id=previous.run_id,
        step_key=previous.step_key,
        step_type=previous.step_type,
        sequence=previous.sequence,
        script_version_id=previous.script_version_id,
        status="pending",
        attempt_no=next_number,
        idempotency_key=(
            f"{run.run_key}:{previous.step_key}:{callback_payload_hash}:"
            f"attempt:{next_number}"
        ),
        input_manifest_json=canonical_json(callback_payload),
        input_hash=callback_payload_hash,
        output_manifest_json="{}",
        checkpoint_json="{}",
        last_error_code="",
        last_error_message="",
    )
    db.add(next_attempt)
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        RuntimeDispatchItem(
            step_attempt_id=next_attempt.id,
            queue_class=run.queue_class,
            priority=50,
            status="queued",
            available_at=now,
            max_attempts=script.max_attempts,
        )
    )
    return next_attempt


def resume_external_wait(
    db: Session,
    request: ExternalResumeRequest,
) -> ProductionRun:
    correlation_id = _required(
        request.correlation_id,
        name="回调关联号",
        maximum=200,
    )
    if request.callback_intake_event_id < 1:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_callback_invalid",
            "回调事实标识必须是正整数",
            status_code=400,
        )
    callback_hash = _hash_shape(request.callback_payload_hash)
    _required(request.actor, name="回调接入方", maximum=80)
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    wait = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowExternalWait)
        .where(WorkflowExternalWait.correlation_id == correlation_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if wait is None:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_not_found",
            "没有找到对应的外部等待事实",
            status_code=404,
        )
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == wait.production_run_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if run is None:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_run_missing",
            "等待关联的运行不存在",
        )
    if wait.state == "resumed":
        if (
            wait.callback_intake_event_id == request.callback_intake_event_id
            and wait.callback_payload_hash == callback_hash
        ):
            return run
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_callback_conflict",
            "该等待已被另一条回调事实恢复",
        )
    if wait.state != "waiting" or run.status != "waiting_external":
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_state_invalid",
            "当前等待已经结束，不能再次恢复",
        )
    callback = db.scalar(
        select(UpstreamIntakeEvent)
        .where(UpstreamIntakeEvent.id == request.callback_intake_event_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if callback is None or callback.disposition not in {"accepted", "replayed"}:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_callback_invalid",
            "回调事实不存在或尚不可运行",
        )
    if callback.contract_version_id != wait.expected_contract_version_id:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_callback_contract_mismatch",
            "回调事实没有使用等待时冻结的合同版本",
        )
    if callback.category_key != run.category_key:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_callback_category_mismatch",
            "回调事实类目与等待中的运行不一致",
        )
    if callback.payload_hash != callback_hash:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_callback_hash_mismatch",
            "回调内容摘要与接入事实不一致",
        )
    callback_payload = _callback_payload(callback)
    if callback.production_run_id not in {None, run.id}:
        raise ExternalWaitError(
            "external_wait_callback_run_conflict",
            "回调事实已经绑定其他运行",
        )

    current = _now()
    callback.production_run_id = run.id
    resumed_attempt = _schedule_same_node_callback_attempt(
        db,
        run=run,
        wait=wait,
        callback_payload=callback_payload,
        callback_payload_hash=callback.payload_hash,
        now=current,
    )
    wait.state = "resumed"
    wait.callback_intake_event_id = callback.id
    wait.callback_payload_hash = callback.payload_hash
    wait.resumed_at = current
    from .incremental_sla import ensure_sla_projection
    ensure_sla_projection(run)  # 迁移 131 前进入外部等待的旧 run，回调重排时补投影（复核 F1）
    run.status = "queued"
    run.current_step_key = wait.resume_node_instance_id
    run.error_code = ""
    run.error_message = ""
    run.blockers_json = "[]"
    run.lease_owner = None
    run.lease_token = None
    run.lease_expires_at = None
    run.heartbeat_at = None
    db.flush()

    from .workflow_runtime import _reconcile_locked_runtime_run

    _reconcile_locked_runtime_run(db, run, now=current)
    if resumed_attempt is not None:
        run.status = "queued"
        run.current_step_key = resumed_attempt.step_key
        run.lease_owner = None
        run.lease_token = None
        run.lease_expires_at = None
        run.heartbeat_at = None
        db.flush()
    return run


def expire_external_waits(
    db: Session,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
    limit: int = 100,
) -> ExternalWaitExpirySummary:
    if not 1 <= limit <= 1000:
        raise ValueError("limit 必须在 1 到 1000 之间")
    current = _now(now)
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    waits = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowExternalWait)
        .where(
            WorkflowExternalWait.state == "waiting",
            WorkflowExternalWait.deadline_at <= current,
        )
        .order_by(WorkflowExternalWait.deadline_at, WorkflowExternalWait.id)
        .limit(limit)
        .with_for_update(skip_locked=True)
    ).all()
    manual_review_count = 0
    failed_count = 0
    wait_ids: list[int] = []
    for wait in waits:
        run = db.scalar(
            select(ProductionRun)
            .where(ProductionRun.id == wait.production_run_id)
            .with_for_update()
            .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
        )
        if run is None or run.status != "waiting_external":
            continue
        wait.state = "expired"
        wait.expired_at = current
        wait_ids.append(wait.id)
        if wait.timeout_policy == "manual_review":
            manual_review_count += 1
            run.status = "blocked"
            run.current_step_key = wait.resume_node_instance_id
            run.error_code = "external_wait_manual_review"
            run.error_message = "等待外部系统超时，需要人工确认后续处理"
            run.blockers_json = canonical_json(
                [
                    {
                        "code": "external_wait_manual_review",
                        "message": run.error_message,
                        "step_key": wait.resume_node_instance_id,
                        "continuation_key": wait.continuation_key,
                    }
                ]
            )
            run.lease_owner = None
            run.lease_token = None
            run.lease_expires_at = None
            run.heartbeat_at = None
        else:
            failed_count += 1
            db.flush()
            from .workflow_runtime import terminalize_runtime_run

            terminalize_runtime_run(
                db,
                run,
                status="failed",
                error_code="external_wait_timed_out",
                error_message="等待外部系统超时，运行已按冻结策略失败",
                now=current,
            )
    db.flush()
    return ExternalWaitExpirySummary(
        expired_count=len(wait_ids),
        manual_review_count=manual_review_count,
        failed_count=failed_count,
        wait_ids=tuple(wait_ids),
    )
